39 /**
40 * @brief Transform the memory layout of an nda::basic_array or nda::basic_array_view.
41 *
42 * @details Depending on the input type `A`, the function does the following:
43 * - If `A` is a regular rvalue array, return a new regular array with the given layout.
44 * - Otherwise it returns a view with the given layout and
45 * - the same value type as `A` if `A` is non-const.
46 * - the const value type of `A` if `A` is const.
47 *
48 * The algebra of the transformed array/view is the same as the input array/view, except if its rank is different.
49 * Then the algebra is set to 'A'.
50 *
51 * @tparam A nda::MemoryArray type.
52 * @tparam NewLayoutType nda::idx_map type.
53 * @param a Array/View to transform.
54 * @param new_layout New memory layout.
55 * @return If the input is an rvalue array, return a new array with the given layout. Otherwise, return a view with
56 * the given layout.
57 */
58 template <MemoryArray A, typename NewLayoutType>
59 8482 auto map_layout_transform([[maybe_unused]] A &&a, [[maybe_unused]] NewLayoutType const &new_layout) {
60 using A_t = std::remove_reference_t<A>;
61
62 // layout policy of transformed array/view
63 using layout_policy = typename detail::layout_to_policy<NewLayoutType>::type;
64
65 // algebra of transformed array/view
66 static constexpr auto algebra = (NewLayoutType::rank() == get_rank<A> ? get_algebra<A> : 'A');
67
68 if constexpr (is_regular_v<A> and !std::is_reference_v<A>) {
69 // return a transformed array if the given type is an rvalue array
70 using array_t = basic_array<typename A_t::value_type, NewLayoutType::rank(), layout_policy, algebra, typename A_t::container_policy_t>;
71 6 return array_t{new_layout, std::forward<A>(a).storage()};
72 } else {
73 // otherwise return a transformed view
74 using value_t = std::conditional_t<std::is_const_v<A_t>, const typename A_t::value_type, typename A_t::value_type>;
75 using accessor_policy = typename get_view_t<A>::accessor_policy_t;
76 using owning_policy = typename get_view_t<A>::owning_policy_t;
77 8476 return basic_array_view<value_t, NewLayoutType::rank(), layout_policy, algebra, accessor_policy, owning_policy>{new_layout, a.storage()};
78 }
79 }
80
81 /**
82 * @brief Reshape an nda::basic_array or nda::basic_array_view.
83 *
84 * @details The input array/view is assumed to be contiguous and in C- or Fortran-order and the size of the reshaped
85 * array/view must be the same as the input.
86 *
87 * It calls nda::map_layout_transform with the given shape.
88 *
89 * @tparam A nda::MemoryArray type.
90 * @tparam Int Integral type.
91 * @tparam R Number of dimensions of the new shape.
92 * @param a Array/View to reshape.
93 * @param new_shape Shape of the reshaped array/view.
94 * @return An array/view with the new shape.

211 /**
212 * @brief Create a new nda::basic_array or nda::basic_array_view by grouping indices together of a given array/view.
213 *
214 * @details The resulting array/view has the same number of dimensions as given index groups.
215 *
216 * It first calls nda::group_indices_layout to create a new nda::idx_map with the grouped indices and then calls
217 * nda::map_layout_transform with the new layout.
218 *
219 * It can be used as follows:
220 *
221 * @code{.cpp}
222 * auto arr = nda::rand<double>(3, 4, 5, 6);
223 * auto view = nda::group_indices_view(arr, idx_group<0, 1>, idx_group<2, 3>);
224 * @endcode
225 *
226 * In this examples a 4-dimensional array `arr` is transformed into a 2-dimensional view by grouping the first and
227 * the last two indices together.
228 *
229 * @tparam A nda::MemoryArray type.
230 * @tparam IdxGrps Groups of indices.
231 * @param a Array/View to transform.
232 * @return An array/view with grouped indices.
233 */
234 template <MemoryArray A, typename... IdxGrps>
235 14 auto group_indices_view(A &&a, IdxGrps...) {

239 namespace detail {

241 // Append N fast dimensions to a given stride order.s
242 template <int N, auto R>
243 constexpr std::array<int, R + N> complete_stride_order_with_fast(std::array<int, R> const &order) {
244 auto r = stdutil::make_initialized_array<R + N>(0);
245 for (int i = 0; i < R; ++i) r[i] = order[i];
246 for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) r[R + i] = R + i;
247 return r;
248 }
249
250 } // namespace detail

252 /**
253 * @brief Add `N` fast varying dimensions of size 1 to a given nda::basic_array or nda::basic_array_view.
254 *
255 * @tparam N Number of dimensions to add.
256 * @tparam A nda::MemoryArray type.
257 * @param a Array/View to transform.
258 * @return An array/view with `N` dimensions of size 1 added to the original array/view.
259 */
260 template <int N, typename A>
261 2 auto reinterpret_add_fast_dims_of_size_one(A &&a)
262 requires(nda::is_regular_or_view_v<A>)
263 {
264 2 auto const &lay = a.indexmap();
265 using lay_t = std::decay_t<decltype(lay)>;
266
267 static constexpr uint64_t new_stride_order_encoded = encode(detail::complete_stride_order_with_fast<N>(lay_t::stride_order));
268 static constexpr uint64_t new_static_extents_encoded = encode(stdutil::join(lay_t::static_extents, stdutil::make_initialized_array<N>(0)));
269 using new_lay_t = idx_map<get_rank<A> + N, new_static_extents_encoded, new_stride_order_encoded, lay_t::layout_prop>;
270
271 2 auto ones_n = stdutil::make_initialized_array<N>(1l);
272 2 return map_layout_transform(std::forward<A>(a), new_lay_t{stdutil::join(lay.lengths(), ones_n), stdutil::join(lay.strides(), ones_n)});
273 }
